   372	
   373	const positionEdges = [
   374		['top', Yoga.EDGE_TOP],
   375		['right', Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT],
   376		['bottom', Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM],
   377		['left', Yoga.EDGE_LEFT],
   378	] as const;
   379	
   380	const applyPositionStyles = (node: YogaNode, style: Styles): void => {
   381		if ('position' in style) {
   382			let positionType = Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_RELATIVE;
   383	
   384			if (style.position === 'absolute') {
   385				positionType = Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE;
   386			} else if (style.position === 'static') {
   387				positionType = Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_STATIC;
   388			}
   389	
   390			node.setPositionType(positionType);
   391		}
   392	
   393		for (const [property, edge] of positionEdges) {
   394			if (!(property in style)) {
   395				continue;
   396			}
   397	
   398			const value = style[property];
   399	
   400			if (typeof value === 'string') {
   401				node.setPositionPercent(edge, Number.parseFloat(value));
   402				continue;
   403			}
   404	
   405			node.setPosition(edge, value);
   406		}
   407	};
   408	
   409	const applyMarginStyles = (node: YogaNode, style: Styles): void => {
   410		if ('margin' in style) {
   411			node.setMargin(Yoga.EDGE_ALL, style.margin ?? 0);
   412		}
   413	
   414		if ('marginX' in style) {
   415			node.setMargin(Yoga.EDGE_HORIZONTAL, style.marginX ?? 0);
   416		}
   417	
   418		if ('marginY' in style) {
   419			node.setMargin(Yoga.EDGE_VERTICAL, style.marginY ?? 0);
   420		}

     1	import widestLine from 'widest-line';

     9	import {type DOMElement} from './dom.js';
    10	import type Output from './output.js';
    11	
    12	// If parent container is `<Box>`, text nodes will be treated as separate nodes in
    13	// the tree and will have their own coordinates in the layout.
    14	// To ensure text nodes are aligned correctly, take X and Y of the first text node
    15	// and use it as offset for the rest of the nodes
    16	// Only first node is taken into account, because other text nodes can't have margin or padding,
    17	// so their coordinates will be relative to the first node anyway
    18	const applyPaddingToText = (node: DOMElement, text: string): string => {
    19		const yogaNode = node.childNodes[0]?.yogaNode;
    20	
    21		if (yogaNode) {
    22			const offsetX = yogaNode.getComputedLeft();
    23			const offsetY = yogaNode.getComputedTop();
    24			text = '\n'.repeat(offsetY) + indentString(text, offsetX);
    25		}
    26	
    27		return text;
    28	};
    29	
    30	export type OutputTransformer = (s: string, index: number) => string;
    31	
    32	export const renderNodeToScreenReaderOutput = (
    33		node: DOMElement,
    34		options: {
    35			parentRole?: string;
    36			skipStaticElements?: boolean;
    37		} = {},
    38	): string => {
    39		if (options.skipStaticElements && node.internal_static) {
    40			return '';
    41		}
    42	
    43		if (node.yogaNode?.getDisplay() === Yoga.DISPLAY_NONE) {
    44			return '';
    45		}
    46	
    47		let output = '';
    48	
    49		if (node.nodeName === 'ink-text') {
    50			output = squashTextNodes(node);
    51		} else if (node.nodeName === 'ink-box' || node.nodeName === 'ink-root') {
    52			const separator =
    53				node.style.flexDirection === 'row' ||
    54				node.style.flexDirection === 'row-reverse'
    55					? ' '
    56					: '\n';
    57	
    58			const childNodes =
    59				node.style.flexDirection === 'row-reverse' ||
    60				node.style.flexDirection === 'column-reverse'
    61					? [...node.childNodes].reverse()
    62					: [...node.childNodes];
    63	
    64			output = childNodes
    65				.map(childNode => {
    66					const screenReaderOutput = renderNodeToScreenReaderOutput(
    67						childNode as DOMElement,
    68						{
    69							parentRole: node.internal_accessibility?.role,
    70							skipStaticElements: options.skipStaticElements,
    71						},
    72					);
    73					return screenReaderOutput;
    74				})
    75				.filter(Boolean)
    76				.join(separator);
    77		}
    78	
    79		if (node.internal_accessibility) {
    80			const {role, state} = node.internal_accessibility;
    81	
    82			if (state) {
    83				const stateKeys = Object.keys(state) as Array<keyof typeof state>;
    84				const stateDescription = stateKeys.filter(key => state[key]).join(', ');
    85	
    86				if (stateDescription) {
    87					output = `(${stateDescription}) ${output}`;
    88				}
    89			}
    90	
    91			if (role && role !== options.parentRole) {
    92				output = `${role}: ${output}`;
    93			}
    94		}
    95	
    96		return output;
    97	};
    98	
    99	// After nodes are laid out, render each to output object, which later gets rendered to terminal
   100	const renderNodeToOutput = (
   101		node: DOMElement,
   102		output: Output,
   103		options: {
   104			offsetX?: number;
   105			offsetY?: number;
   106			transformers?: OutputTransformer[];
   107			skipStaticElements: boolean;
   108		},
   109	) => {
   110		const {
   111			offsetX = 0,
   112			offsetY = 0,
   113			transformers = [],
   114			skipStaticElements,
   115		} = options;
   116	
   117		if (skipStaticElements && node.internal_static) {
   118			return;
   119		}
   120	
   121		const {yogaNode} = node;
   122	
   123		if (yogaNode) {
   124			if (yogaNode.getDisplay() === Yoga.DISPLAY_NONE) {
   125				return;
   126			}
   127	
   128			// Left and top positions in Yoga are relative to their parent node
   129			const x = offsetX + yogaNode.getComputedLeft();
   130			const y = offsetY + yogaNode.getComputedTop();
   131	
   132			// Transformers are functions that transform final text output of each component
   133			// See Output class for logic that applies transformers
   134			let newTransformers = transformers;
   135	
   136			if (typeof node.internal_transform === 'function') {
   137				newTransformers = [node.internal_transform, ...transformers];
   138			}
   139	
   140			if (node.nodeName === 'ink-text') {
   141				let text = squashTextNodes(node);
   142	
   143				if (text.length > 0) {
   144					const currentWidth = widestLine(text);
   145					const maxWidth = getMaxWidth(yogaNode);
   146	
   147					if (currentWidth > maxWidth) {
   148						const textWrap = node.style.textWrap ?? 'wrap';
   149						text = wrapText(text, maxWidth, textWrap);
   150					}
   151	
   152					text = applyPaddingToText(node, text);
   153	
   154					output.write(x, y, text, {transformers: newTransformers});
   155				}
   156	
   157				return;
   158			}
   159	
   160			let clipped = false;
   161	
   162			if (node.nodeName === 'ink-box') {
   163				renderBackground(x, y, node, output);
   164				renderBorder(x, y, node, output);
   165	
   166				const clipHorizontally =
   167					node.style.overflowX === 'hidden' || node.style.overflow === 'hidden';
   168				const clipVertically =
   169					node.style.overflowY === 'hidden' || node.style.overflow === 'hidden';
   170	
   171				if (clipHorizontally || clipVertically) {
   172					const x1 = clipHorizontally
   173						? x + yogaNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT)
   174						: undefined;
   175	
   176					const x2 = clipHorizontally
   177						? x +
   178							yogaNode.getComputedWidth() -
   179							yogaNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT)
   180						: undefined;
   181	
   182					const y1 = clipVertically
   183						? y + yogaNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_TOP)
   184						: undefined;
   185	
   186					const y2 = clipVertically
   187						? y +
   188							yogaNode.getComputedHeight() -
   189							yogaNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM)
   190						: undefined;
   191	
   192					output.clip({x1, x2, y1, y2});
   193					clipped = true;
   194				}
   195			}
   196	
   197			if (node.nodeName === 'ink-root' || node.nodeName === 'ink-box') {
   198				for (const childNode of node.childNodes) {
   199					renderNodeToOutput(childNode as DOMElement, output, {
   200						offsetX: x,

     1	import {type DOMElement} from './dom.js';
     2	
     3	type Output = {
     4		/**
     5		Element width.
     6		*/
     7		width: number;
     8	
     9		/**
    10		Element height.
    11		*/
    12		height: number;
    13	};
    14	
    15	/**
    16	Measure the dimensions of a particular `<Box>` element.
    17	Returns an object with `width` and `height` properties.
    18	This function is useful when your component needs to know the amount of available space it has. You can use it when you need to change the layout based on the length of its content.
    19	
    20	Note: `measureElement()` returns `{width: 0, height: 0}` when called during render (before layout is calculated). Call it from post-render code, such as `useEffect`, `useLayoutEffect`, input handlers, or timer callbacks. When content changes, pass the relevant dependency to your effect so it re-measures after each update.
    21	*/
    22	const measureElement = (node: DOMElement): Output => ({
    23		width: node.yogaNode?.getComputedWidth() ?? 0,
    24		height: node.yogaNode?.getComputedHeight() ?? 0,
    25	});
    26	
    27	export default measureElement;

   111

   118		@default true (false if in CI or `stdout.isTTY` is falsy)
   119		*/
   120		interactive?: boolean;
   121	
   122		/**
   123		Render the app in the terminal's alternate screen buffer. When enabled, the app renders on a separate screen, and the original terminal content is restored when the app exits. This is the same mechanism used by programs like vim, htop, and less.
   124	
   125		Note: The terminal's scrollback buffer is not available while in the alternate screen. This is standard terminal behavior; programs like vim use the alternate screen specifically to avoid polluting the user's scrollback history.
   126	
   127		Note: Ink intentionally treats alternate-screen teardown output as disposable. It does not preserve or replay teardown-time frames, hook writes, or `console.*` output after restoring the primary screen.
   128	
   129		Only works in interactive mode. Ignored when `interactive` is `false` or in a non-interactive environment (CI, piped stdout).
   130	
   131		Note: Reusing the same stdout across multiple `render()` calls without unmounting is unsupported. Call `unmount()` first if you need to change this option or create a fresh instance.
   132	
   133		@default false
   134		*/
   135		alternateScreen?: boolean;
   136	};
   137	
   138	export type Instance = {
   139		/**
   140		Replace the previous root node with a new one or update props of the current root node.
   141		*/
   142		rerender: Ink['render'];
   143	
   144		/**
   145		Manually unmount the whole Ink app.
   146		*/
   147		unmount: Ink['unmount'];
   148	
   149		/**
   150		Returns a promise that settles when the app is unmounted.
   151	
   152		It resolves with the value passed to `exit(value)` and rejects with the error passed to `exit(error)`.
   153		When `unmount()` is called manually, it settles after unmount-related stdout writes complete.
   154	
   155		@example
   156		```jsx
   157		const {unmount, waitUntilExit} = render(<MyApp />);
   158	
   159		setTimeout(unmount, 1000);
   160	
   161		await waitUntilExit(); // resolves after `unmount()` is called
   162		```
   163		*/
   164		waitUntilExit: Ink['waitUntilExit'];
   165	
   166		/**
   167		Returns a promise that settles after pending render output is flushed to stdout.
   168	
   169		This can be used after `rerender()` when you need to run code only after the frame is written.
   170	
   171		@example
   172		```jsx
   173		const {rerender, waitUntilRenderFlush} = render(<MyApp step="loading" />);
   174	
   175		rerender(<MyApp step="ready" />);
   176		await waitUntilRenderFlush(); // output for "ready" is flushed
   177	
   178		runNextCommand();
   179		```
   180		*/
   181		waitUntilRenderFlush: Ink['waitUntilRenderFlush'];
   182	
   183		/**
   184		Unmount the current app and remove the internal Ink instance for this stdout.
   185	
   186		This is mostly useful for advanced cases where you need `render()` to create a fresh instance for the same stream without leaving terminal state such as the alternate screen behind.
   187		*/
   188		cleanup: () => void;
   189	
   190		/**
   191		Clear output.
   192		*/
   193		clear: () => void;
   194	};
   195	
   196	/**
   197	Mount a component and render the output.
   198	*/
   199	const render = (
   200		node: ReactNode,
   201		options?: NodeJS.WriteStream | RenderOptions,
   202	): Instance => {
   203		const inkOptions: InkOptions = {
   204			stdout: process.stdout,
   205			stdin: process.stdin,
   206			stderr: process.stderr,
   207			debug: false,
   208			exitOnCtrlC: true,
   209			patchConsole: true,
   210			maxFps: 30,
   211			incrementalRendering: false,
   212			concurrent: false,
   213			alternateScreen: false,
   214			...getOptions(options),
   215		};
   216	
   217		const instance: Ink = getInstance(
   218			inkOptions.stdout,
   219			() => new Ink(inkOptions),
   220		);

     1	import React, {forwardRef, useContext, type PropsWithChildren} from 'react';
     2	import {type Except} from 'type-fest';
     3	import {type Styles} from '../styles.js';
     4	import {type DOMElement} from '../dom.js';
     5	import {accessibilityContext} from './AccessibilityContext.js';
     6	import {backgroundContext} from './BackgroundContext.js';
     7	
     8	export type Props = Except<Styles, 'textWrap'> & {
     9		/**
    10		A label for the element for screen readers.
    11		*/
    12		readonly 'aria-label'?: string;
    13	
    14		/**
    15		Hide the element from screen readers.
    16		*/
    17		readonly 'aria-hidden'?: boolean;
    18	
    19		/**
    20		The role of the element.
    21		*/
    22		readonly 'aria-role'?:
    23			| 'button'
    24			| 'checkbox'
    25			| 'combobox'
    26			| 'list'
    27			| 'listbox'
    28			| 'listitem'
    29			| 'menu'
    30			| 'menuitem'
    31			| 'option'
    32			| 'progressbar'
    33			| 'radio'
    34			| 'radiogroup'
    35			| 'tab'
    36			| 'tablist'
    37			| 'table'
    38			| 'textbox'
    39			| 'timer'
    40			| 'toolbar';
    41	
    42		/**
    43		The state of the element.
    44		*/
    45		readonly 'aria-state'?: {
    46			readonly busy?: boolean;
    47			readonly checked?: boolean;
    48			readonly disabled?: boolean;
    49			readonly expanded?: boolean;
    50			readonly multiline?: boolean;
    51			readonly multiselectable?: boolean;
    52			readonly readonly?: boolean;
    53			readonly required?: boolean;
    54			readonly selected?: boolean;
    55		};
    56	};
    57	
    58	/**
    59	`<Box>` is an essential Ink component to build your layout. It's like `<div style="display: flex">` in the browser.
    60	*/
    61	const Box = forwardRef<DOMElement, PropsWithChildren<Props>>(
    62		(
    63			{
    64				children,
    65				backgroundColor,
    66				'aria-label': ariaLabel,
    67				'aria-hidden': ariaHidden,
    68				'aria-role': role,
    69				'aria-state': ariaState,
    70				...style
    71			},
    72			ref,
    73		) => {
    74			const {isScreenReaderEnabled} = useContext(accessibilityContext);
    75			const label = ariaLabel ? <ink-text>{ariaLabel}</ink-text> : undefined;
    76			if (isScreenReaderEnabled && ariaHidden) {
    77				return null;
    78			}
    79	
    80			const boxElement = (
    81				<ink-box
    82					ref={ref}
    83					style={{
    84						flexWrap: 'nowrap',
    85						flexDirection: 'row',
    86						flexGrow: 0,
    87						flexShrink: 1,
    88						...style,
    89						backgroundColor,
    90						overflowX: style.overflowX ?? style.overflow ?? 'visible',
    91						overflowY: style.overflowY ?? style.overflow ?? 'visible',
    92					}}
    93					internal_accessibility={{
    94						role,
    95						state: ariaState,
    96					}}
    97				>
    98					{isScreenReaderEnabled && label ? label : children}
    99				</ink-box>
   100			);
   101	
   102			// If this Box has a background color, provide it to children via context
   103			if (backgroundColor) {
   104				return (
   105					<backgroundContext.Provider value={backgroundColor}>
   106						{boxElement}
   107					</backgroundContext.Provider>
   108				);
   109			}
   110	
   111			return boxElement;
   112		},
   113	);
   114	
   115	Box.displayName = 'Box';
   116	
   117	export default Box;

   111	export const appendChildNode = (
   112		node: DOMElement,
   113		childNode: DOMElement,
   114	): void => {
   115		if (childNode.parentNode) {
   116			removeChildNode(childNode.parentNode, childNode);
   117		}
   118	
   119		childNode.parentNode = node;
   120		node.childNodes.push(childNode);
   121	
   122		if (childNode.yogaNode) {
   123			node.yogaNode?.insertChild(
   124				childNode.yogaNode,
   125				node.yogaNode.getChildCount(),
   126			);
   127		}
   128	
   129		if (node.nodeName === 'ink-text' || node.nodeName === 'ink-virtual-text') {
   130			markNodeAsDirty(node);
   131		}
   132	};

     1	import {act} from 'react';
     2	import {render} from '../../src/index.js';
     3	import createStdout from './create-stdout.js';
     4	
     5	type RenderToStringOptions = {
     6		columns?: number;
     7		isScreenReaderEnabled?: boolean;
     8	};
     9	
    10	/**
    11	Synchronous render to string (legacy mode).
    12	*/
    13	export const renderToString: (
    14		node: React.JSX.Element,
    15		options?: RenderToStringOptions,
    16	) => string = (node, options) => {
    17		const stdout = createStdout(options?.columns ?? 100);
    18	
    19		render(node, {
    20			stdout,
    21			debug: true,
    22			isScreenReaderEnabled: options?.isScreenReaderEnabled,
    23		});
    24	
    25		const output = stdout.get();
    26		return output;
    27	};
    28	
    29	/**
    30	Async render to string with concurrent mode support.
    31	
    32	Uses `act()` to properly flush updates.
    33	*/
    34	export const renderToStringAsync: (
    35		node: React.JSX.Element,
    36		options?: RenderToStringOptions,
    37	) => Promise<string> = async (node, options) => {
    38		const stdout = createStdout(options?.columns ?? 100);
    39	
    40		await act(async () => {
    41			render(node, {
    42				stdout,
    43				debug: true,
    44				isScreenReaderEnabled: options?.isScreenReaderEnabled,
    45				concurrent: true,
    46			});
    47		});
    48	
    49		const output = stdout.get();
    50		return output;
    51	};

import Yoga from 'yoga-layout';
function test(pad, border, childPos, childMargin) {
  const parent = Yoga.Node.create();
  parent.setWidth(30); parent.setHeight(10);
  if (pad) parent.setPadding(Yoga.EDGE_ALL, pad);
  if (border) parent.setBorder(Yoga.EDGE_ALL, border);
  const child = Yoga.Node.create();
  child.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE);
  child.setPosition(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT, childPos);
  child.setPosition(Yoga.EDGE_TOP, childPos);
  if (childMargin) { child.setMargin(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT, childMargin); child.setMargin(Yoga.EDGE_TOP, childMargin); }
  child.setWidth(5); child.setHeight(4);
  parent.insertChild(child, 0);
  parent.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
  console.log({pad, border, childPos, childMargin, computedLeft: child.getComputedLeft(), computedTop: child.getComputedTop()});
}
test(0,0,5,0);
test(2,1,3,0);

test(2,1,3,2);
test(0,0,5,1);

STEP 26 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > src/grid.ts <<'EOF'
import Yoga, {type Node as YogaNode} from 'yoga-layout';
import {type DOMElement} from './dom.js';
import {type Styles} from './styles.js';

type Track =
	| {kind: 'fixed'; value: number}
	| {kind: 'auto'}
	| {kind: 'fr'; value: number}
	| {kind: 'minmax'; min: number; max: number}
	| {kind: 'minmax-fr'; min: number; fr: number};

type GridPlacement = {
	start: number;
	end: number;
};

type GridItem = {
	child: DOMElement;
	column: GridPlacement | undefined;
	row: GridPlacement | undefined;
	contentWidth: number;
	contentHeight: number;
	marginLeft: number;
	marginRight: number;
	marginTop: number;
	marginBottom: number;
	columnStart: number;
	columnEnd: number;
	rowStart: number;
	rowEnd: number;
};

const parseTrack = (token: string): Track | undefined => {
	const value = token.trim();

	if (value === 'auto') {
		return {kind: 'auto'};
	}

	if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(value)) {
		return {kind: 'fixed', value: Number(value)};
	}

	if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?fr$/.test(value)) {
		return {kind: 'fr', value: Number.parseFloat(value)};
	}

	if (value.startsWith('minmax(') && value.endsWith(')')) {
		const [minValue, maxValue] = value
			.slice('minmax('.length, -1)
			.split(',')
			.map(part => part.trim());

		const min = Number(minValue);

		if (maxValue.endsWith('fr')) {
			return {kind: 'minmax-fr', min, fr: Number.parseFloat(maxValue)};
		}

		return {kind: 'minmax', min, max: Number(maxValue)};
	}

	return undefined;
};

const parseTrackList = (value?: string): Track[] => {
	if (!value) {
		return [];
	}

	return value
		.trim()
		.split(/\s+/)
		.map(parseTrack)
		.filter((track): track is Track => Boolean(track));
};

const parseGridLine = (value?: number | string): GridPlacement | undefined => {
	if (value === undefined) {
		return undefined;
	}

	if (typeof value === 'number') {
		return {start: value, end: value + 1};
	}

	const parts = value.split('/').map(part => part.trim());

	if (parts.length === 1) {
		const start = Number(parts[0]);
		return {start, end: start + 1};
	}

	return {start: Number(parts[0]), end: Number(parts[1])};
};

const getMargin = (
	style: Styles,
	axis: 'left' | 'right' | 'top' | 'bottom',
): number => {
	const margin = style.margin ?? 0;

	switch (axis) {
		case 'left':
			return style.marginLeft ?? style.marginX ?? margin;
		case 'right':
			return style.marginRight ?? style.marginX ?? margin;
		case 'top':
			return style.marginTop ?? style.marginY ?? margin;
		case 'bottom':
			return style.marginBottom ?? style.marginY ?? margin;
	}
};

const isGridContainer = (node: DOMElement): boolean =>
	node.nodeName !== '#text' && Boolean(node.yogaNode) && node.style.display === 'grid';

const collectGridContainers = (node: DOMElement, containers: DOMElement[] = []): DOMElement[] => {
	if (isGridContainer(node)) {
		containers.push(node);
	}

	for (const child of node.childNodes) {
		if (child.yogaNode) {
			collectGridContainers(child, containers);
		}
	}

	return containers;
};

const isOccupied = (
	occupied: Set<string>,
	rowStart: number,
	rowEnd: number,
	columnStart: number,
	columnEnd: number,
): boolean => {
	for (let row = rowStart; row < rowEnd; row++) {
		for (let column = columnStart; column < columnEnd; column++) {
			if (occupied.has(`${row}:${column}`)) {
				return true;
			}
		}
	}

	return false;
};

const occupy = (
	occupied: Set<string>,
	rowStart: number,
	rowEnd: number,
	columnStart: number,
	columnEnd: number,
): void => {
	for (let row = rowStart; row < rowEnd; row++) {
		for (let column = columnStart; column < columnEnd; column++) {
			occupied.add(`${row}:${column}`);
		}
	}
};

const determinePlacements = (items: GridItem[], initialColumnCount: number): {items: GridItem[]; columnCount: number; rowCount: number} => {
	const occupied = new Set<string>();
	let columnCount = initialColumnCount;
	let cursorRow = 0;
	let cursorColumn = 0;

	for (const item of items) {
		const {column, row} = item;
		let columnStart: number;
		let columnEnd: number;
		let rowStart: number;
		let rowEnd: number;

		if (column) {
			columnStart = column.start - 1;
			columnEnd = column.end - 1;
			columnCount = Math.max(columnCount, columnEnd);
		}

		if (row) {
			rowStart = row.start - 1;
			rowEnd = row.end - 1;
		}

		if (column && row) {
			// Explicit placement
		} else if (column) {
			rowStart = 0;
			rowEnd = 1;

			while (isOccupied(occupied, rowStart, rowEnd, columnStart, columnEnd)) {
				rowStart++;
				rowEnd++;
			}
		} else if (row) {
			columnStart = 0;
			columnEnd = 1;

			while (isOccupied(occupied, rowStart, rowEnd, columnStart, columnEnd)) {
				columnStart++;
				columnEnd++;

				if (columnStart >= columnCount) {
					columnStart = 0;
					columnEnd = 1;
					rowStart++;
					rowEnd++;
				}
			}
		} else {
			columnStart = cursorColumn;
			columnEnd = cursorColumn + 1;

			while (columnEnd > columnCount) {
				cursorRow++;
				cursorColumn = 0;
				columnStart = 0;
				columnEnd = 1;
			}

			while (isOccupied(occupied, cursorRow, cursorRow + 1, columnStart, columnEnd)) {
				cursorColumn++;
				columnStart++;
				columnEnd++;

				if (columnEnd > columnCount) {
					cursorRow++;
					cursorColumn = 0;
					columnStart = 0;
					columnEnd = 1;
				}
			}

			rowStart = cursorRow;
			rowEnd = cursorRow + 1;
		}

		item.columnStart = columnStart;
		item.columnEnd = columnEnd;
		item.rowStart = rowStart;
		item.rowEnd = rowEnd;

		occupy(occupied, rowStart, rowEnd, columnStart, columnEnd);

		if (!column) {
			cursorColumn = columnEnd;
			if (cursorColumn >= columnCount) {
				cursorRow++;
				cursorColumn = 0;
			}
		}
	}

	let rowCount = 0;

	for (const item of items) {
		rowCount = Math.max(rowCount, item.rowEnd);
		columnCount = Math.max(columnCount, item.columnEnd);
	}

	return {items, columnCount, rowCount};
};

const resolveTrackSizes = (
	tracks: Track[],
	items: GridItem[],
	trackCount: number,
	axis: 'column' | 'row',
	gap: number,
	availableSize: number,
	hasExplicitSize: boolean,
): number[] => {
	const normalizedTracks: Track[] = [];

	for (let index = 0; index < trackCount; index++) {
		let track = tracks[index] ?? {kind: 'auto' as const};

		// `fr` tracks can't resolve against an auto-sized container.
		if (!hasExplicitSize && track.kind === 'fr') {
			track = {kind: 'auto'};
		}

		if (!hasExplicitSize && track.kind === 'minmax-fr') {
			track = {kind: 'minmax', min: track.min, max: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY};
		}

		normalizedTracks.push(track);
	}

	const sizes = normalizedTracks.map(track => {
		switch (track.kind) {
			case 'fixed':
				return track.value;
			case 'auto':
				return 0;
			case 'fr':
				return 0;
			case 'minmax':
				return track.min;
			case 'minmax-fr':
				return track.min;
		}
	});

	for (const item of items) {
		const start = axis === 'column' ? item.columnStart : item.rowStart;
		const end = axis === 'column' ? item.columnEnd : item.rowEnd;
		const span = Math.max(1, end - start);
		const contentSize =
			(axis === 'column' ? item.contentWidth : item.contentHeight) +
			(axis === 'column'
				? item.marginLeft + item.marginRight
				: item.marginTop + item.marginBottom);

		const contribution = contentSize / span;

		for (let index = start; index < end; index++) {
			const track = normalizedTracks[index];

			if (track.kind === 'auto') {
				sizes[index] = Math.max(sizes[index], contribution);
			}

			if (track.kind === 'minmax') {
				sizes[index] = Math.max(
					track.min,
					Math.min(track.max, contribution),
				);
			}
		}
	}

	const totalBaseSize =
		sizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) + gap * (trackCount - 1);
	const remaining = hasExplicitSize ? availableSize - totalBaseSize : 0;

	const totalFr = normalizedTracks.reduce((sum, track) => {
		if (track.kind === 'fr') {
			return sum + track.value;
		}

		if (track.kind === 'minmax-fr') {
			return sum + track.fr;
		}

		return sum;
	}, 0);

	if (totalFr > 0 && remaining > 0) {
		for (let index = 0; index < normalizedTracks.length; index++) {
			const track = normalizedTracks[index];
			const fr =
				track.kind === 'fr'
					? track.value
					: track.kind === 'minmax-fr'
						? track.fr
						: 0;

			if (fr > 0) {
				sizes[index] += remaining * (fr / totalFr);
			}
		}
	}

	return sizes;
};

const layoutGridContainer = (container: DOMElement): void => {
	const yoga = container.yogaNode;
	const style = container.style;

	if (!yoga) {
		return;
	}

	const items: GridItem[] = container.childNodes
		.filter((child): child is DOMElement => Boolean(child.yogaNode))
		.filter(child => child.yogaNode!.getDisplay() !== Yoga.DISPLAY_NONE)
		.map(child => ({
			child,
			column: parseGridLine(child.style.gridColumn),
			row: parseGridLine(child.style.gridRow),
			contentWidth: child.yogaNode!.getComputedWidth(),
			contentHeight: child.yogaNode!.getComputedHeight(),
			marginLeft: getMargin(child.style, 'left'),
			marginRight: getMargin(child.style, 'right'),
			marginTop: getMargin(child.style, 'top'),
			marginBottom: getMargin(child.style, 'bottom'),
			columnStart: 0,
			columnEnd: 0,
			rowStart: 0,
			rowEnd: 0,
		}));

	if (items.length === 0) {
		return;
	}

	const columnsTemplate = parseTrackList(style.gridTemplateColumns);
	const rowsTemplate = parseTrackList(style.gridTemplateRows);

	const initialColumnCount = Math.max(1, columnsTemplate.length);
	const {items: placedItems, columnCount, rowCount} = determinePlacements(
		items,
		initialColumnCount,
	);

	const borderLeft = yoga.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
	const borderRight = yoga.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
	const borderTop = yoga.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const borderBottom = yoga.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);
	const paddingLeft = yoga.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
	const paddingRight = yoga.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
	const paddingTop = yoga.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const paddingBottom = yoga.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);

	const columnGap = style.columnGap ?? style.gap ?? 0;
	const rowGap = style.rowGap ?? style.gap ?? 0;

	const hasExplicitWidth = 'width' in style;
	const hasExplicitHeight = 'height' in style;

	const containerWidth = Math.max(
		0,
		yoga.getComputedWidth() - borderLeft - borderRight - paddingLeft - paddingRight,
	);
	const containerHeight = Math.max(
		0,
		yoga.getComputedHeight() - borderTop - borderBottom - paddingTop - paddingBottom,
	);

	const columnSizes = resolveTrackSizes(
		columnsTemplate,
		placedItems,
		columnCount,
		'column',
		columnGap,
		containerWidth,
		hasExplicitWidth,
	);

	const rowSizes = resolveTrackSizes(
		rowsTemplate,
		placedItems,
		rowCount,
		'row',
		rowGap,
		containerHeight,
		hasExplicitHeight,
	);

	const totalColumnSize =
		columnSizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) +
		columnGap * (columnCount - 1);
	const totalRowSize =
		rowSizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) + rowGap * (rowCount - 1);

	if (!hasExplicitWidth) {
		yoga.setWidth(totalColumnSize + borderLeft + borderRight + paddingLeft + paddingRight);
	}

	if (!hasExplicitHeight) {
		yoga.setHeight(totalRowSize + borderTop + borderBottom + paddingTop + paddingBottom);
	}

	const columnOffsets: number[] = [0];
	for (let index = 0; index < columnCount; index++) {
		columnOffsets.push(
			columnOffsets[index] + columnSizes[index] + (index < columnCount - 1 ? columnGap : 0),
		);
	}

	const rowOffsets: number[] = [0];
	for (let index = 0; index < rowCount; index++) {
		rowOffsets.push(
			rowOffsets[index] + rowSizes[index] + (index < rowCount - 1 ? rowGap : 0),
		);
	}

	for (const item of placedItems) {
		const childYoga = item.child.yogaNode!;
		const width =
			columnOffsets[item.columnEnd] -
			columnOffsets[item.columnStart] -
			(item.columnEnd - item.columnStart - 1) * columnGap;
		const height =
			rowOffsets[item.rowEnd] -
			rowOffsets[item.rowStart] -
			(item.rowEnd - item.rowStart - 1) * rowGap;

		childYoga.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE);
		childYoga.setPosition(
			Yoga.EDGE_LEFT,
			paddingLeft + columnOffsets[item.columnStart],
		);
		childYoga.setPosition(
			Yoga.EDGE_TOP,
			paddingTop + rowOffsets[item.rowStart],
		);
		childYoga.setWidth(Math.max(0, width - item.marginLeft - item.marginRight));
		childYoga.setHeight(Math.max(0, height - item.marginTop - item.marginBottom));
	}
};

const computeGridLayouts = (node: DOMElement): void => {
	if (isGridContainer(node)) {
		layoutGridContainer(node);
	}

	for (const child of node.childNodes) {
		if (child.yogaNode) {
			computeGridLayouts(child);
		}
	}
};

export const applyGridLayout = (rootNode: DOMElement): void => {
	const rootYoga = rootNode.yogaNode;

	if (!rootYoga) {
		return;
	}

	// Ensure grid children are measured at their natural content size before
	// grid tracks are resolved.
	for (const container of collectGridContainers(rootNode)) {
		const yoga = container.yogaNode!;

		for (let index = 0; index < yoga.getChildCount(); index++) {
			const child = yoga.getChild(index);
			child.setFlexShrink(0);
			child.setFlexGrow(0);
		}
	}

	rootYoga.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);

	// Apply the grid layout and recalculate so explicitly positioned children
	// stay in place and auto-sized containers get their final dimensions.
	computeGridLayouts(rootNode);
	rootYoga.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
	computeGridLayouts(rootNode);
	rootYoga.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
};

export default applyGridLayout;

COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > src/grid.ts <<'EOF'
import Yoga from 'yoga-layout';
import {type DOMElement, type DOMNode} from './dom.js';
import {type Styles} from './styles.js';

type Track =
	| {kind: 'fixed'; value: number}
	| {kind: 'auto'}
	| {kind: 'fr'; value: number}
	| {kind: 'minmax'; min: number; max: number}
	| {kind: 'minmax-fr'; min: number; fr: number};

type GridPlacement = {
	start: number;
	end: number;
};

type GridItem = {
	child: DOMElement;
	column: GridPlacement | undefined;
	row: GridPlacement | undefined;
	contentWidth: number;
	contentHeight: number;
	marginLeft: number;
	marginRight: number;
	marginTop: number;
	marginBottom: number;
	columnStart: number;
	columnEnd: number;
	rowStart: number;
	rowEnd: number;
};

const isDOMElement = (node: DOMNode): node is DOMElement =>
	node.nodeName !== '#text';

const parseTrack = (token: string): Track | undefined => {
	const value = token.trim();

	if (value === 'auto') {
		return {kind: 'auto'};
	}

	if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(value)) {
		return {kind: 'fixed', value: Number(value)};
	}

	if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?fr$/.test(value)) {
		return {kind: 'fr', value: Number.parseFloat(value)};
	}

	if (value.startsWith('minmax(') && value.endsWith(')')) {
		const [minValue, maxValue] = value
			.slice('minmax('.length, -1)
			.split(',')
			.map(part => part.trim());

		if (!minValue || !maxValue) {
			return undefined;
		}

		const min = Number(minValue);

		if (maxValue.endsWith('fr')) {
			return {kind: 'minmax-fr', min, fr: Number.parseFloat(maxValue)};
		}

		return {kind: 'minmax', min, max: Number(maxValue)};
	}

	return undefined;
};

const parseTrackList = (value?: string): Track[] => {
	if (!value) {
		return [];
	}

	return value
		.trim()
		.split(/\s+/)
		.map(parseTrack)
		.filter((track): track is Track => Boolean(track));
};

const parseGridLine = (value?: number | string): GridPlacement | undefined => {
	if (value === undefined) {
		return undefined;
	}

	if (typeof value === 'number') {
		return {start: value, end: value + 1};
	}

	const parts = value.split('/').map(part => part.trim());

	if (parts.length === 1) {
		const start = Number(parts[0]);
		return {start, end: start + 1};
	}

	return {start: Number(parts[0]), end: Number(parts[1])};
};

const getMargin = (
	style: Styles,
	axis: 'left' | 'right' | 'top' | 'bottom',
): number => {
	const margin = style.margin ?? 0;

	switch (axis) {
		case 'left':
			return style.marginLeft ?? style.marginX ?? margin;
		case 'right':
			return style.marginRight ?? style.marginX ?? margin;
		case 'top':
			return style.marginTop ?? style.marginY ?? margin;
		case 'bottom':
			return style.marginBottom ?? style.marginY ?? margin;
	}
};

const isGridContainer = (node: DOMElement): boolean =>
	Boolean(node.yogaNode) && node.style.display === 'grid';

const collectGridContainers = (
	node: DOMElement,
	containers: DOMElement[] = [],
): DOMElement[] => {
	if (isGridContainer(node)) {
		containers.push(node);
	}

	for (const child of node.childNodes) {
		if (isDOMElement(child) && child.yogaNode) {
			collectGridContainers(child, containers);
		}
	}

	return containers;
};

const isOccupied = (
	occupied: Set<string>,
	rowStart: number,
	rowEnd: number,
	columnStart: number,
	columnEnd: number,
): boolean => {
	for (let row = rowStart; row < rowEnd; row++) {
		for (let column = columnStart; column < columnEnd; column++) {
			if (occupied.has(`${row}:${column}`)) {
				return true;
			}
		}
	}

	return false;
};

const occupy = (
	occupied: Set<string>,
	rowStart: number,
	rowEnd: number,
	columnStart: number,
	columnEnd: number,
): void => {
	for (let row = rowStart; row < rowEnd; row++) {
		for (let column = columnStart; column < columnEnd; column++) {
			occupied.add(`${row}:${column}`);
		}
	}
};

const determinePlacements = (
	items: GridItem[],
	initialColumnCount: number,
): {items: GridItem[]; columnCount: number; rowCount: number} => {
	const occupied = new Set<string>();
	let columnCount = initialColumnCount;
	let cursorRow = 0;
	let cursorColumn = 0;

	for (const item of items) {
		const {column, row} = item;
		let columnStart = 0;
		let columnEnd = 0;
		let rowStart = 0;
		let rowEnd = 0;

		if (column) {
			columnStart = column.start - 1;
			columnEnd = column.end - 1;
			columnCount = Math.max(columnCount, columnEnd);
		}

		if (row) {
			rowStart = row.start - 1;
			rowEnd = row.end - 1;
		}

		if (column && row) {
			// Explicit placement.
		} else if (column) {
			rowStart = 0;
			rowEnd = 1;

			while (isOccupied(occupied, rowStart, rowEnd, columnStart, columnEnd)) {
				rowStart++;
				rowEnd++;
			}
		} else if (row) {
			columnStart = 0;
			columnEnd = 1;

			while (isOccupied(occupied, rowStart, rowEnd, columnStart, columnEnd)) {
				columnStart++;
				columnEnd++;

				if (columnStart >= columnCount) {
					columnStart = 0;
					columnEnd = 1;
					rowStart++;
					rowEnd++;
				}
			}
		} else {
			columnStart = cursorColumn;
			columnEnd = cursorColumn + 1;

			while (columnEnd > columnCount) {
				cursorRow++;
				cursorColumn = 0;
				columnStart = 0;
				columnEnd = 1;
			}

			while (isOccupied(occupied, cursorRow, cursorRow + 1, columnStart, columnEnd)) {
				cursorColumn++;
				columnStart++;
				columnEnd++;

				if (columnEnd > columnCount) {
					cursorRow++;
					cursorColumn = 0;
					columnStart = 0;
					columnEnd = 1;
				}
			}

			rowStart = cursorRow;
			rowEnd = cursorRow + 1;
		}

		item.columnStart = columnStart;
		item.columnEnd = columnEnd;
		item.rowStart = rowStart;
		item.rowEnd = rowEnd;

		occupy(occupied, rowStart, rowEnd, columnStart, columnEnd);

		if (!column) {
			cursorColumn = columnEnd;
			if (cursorColumn >= columnCount) {
				cursorRow++;
				cursorColumn = 0;
			}
		}
	}

	let rowCount = 0;

	for (const item of items) {
		rowCount = Math.max(rowCount, item.rowEnd);
		columnCount = Math.max(columnCount, item.columnEnd);
	}

	return {items, columnCount, rowCount};
};

const resolveTrackSizes = (
	tracks: Track[],
	items: GridItem[],
	trackCount: number,
	axis: 'column' | 'row',
	gap: number,
	availableSize: number,
	hasExplicitSize: boolean,
): number[] => {
	const normalizedTracks: Track[] = [];

	for (let index = 0; index < trackCount; index++) {
		let track = tracks[index] ?? ({kind: 'auto'} as Track);

		// `fr` tracks can't resolve against an auto-sized container.
		if (!hasExplicitSize && track.kind === 'fr') {
			track = {kind: 'auto'};
		}

		if (!hasExplicitSize && track.kind === 'minmax-fr') {
			track = {kind: 'minmax', min: track.min, max: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY};
		}

		normalizedTracks.push(track);
	}

	const sizes: number[] = normalizedTracks.map(track => {
		switch (track.kind) {
			case 'fixed':
				return track.value;
			case 'auto':
				return 0;
			case 'fr':
				return 0;
			case 'minmax':
				return track.min;
			case 'minmax-fr':
				return track.min;
		}
	});

	for (const item of items) {
		const start = axis === 'column' ? item.columnStart : item.rowStart;
		const end = axis === 'column' ? item.columnEnd : item.rowEnd;
		const span = Math.max(1, end - start);
		const contentSize =
			(axis === 'column' ? item.contentWidth : item.contentHeight) +
			(axis === 'column'
				? item.marginLeft + item.marginRight
				: item.marginTop + item.marginBottom);

		const contribution = contentSize / span;

		for (let index = start; index < end; index++) {
			const track = normalizedTracks[index]!;

			if (track.kind === 'auto') {
				sizes[index] = Math.max(sizes[index]!, contribution);
			}

			if (track.kind === 'minmax') {
				sizes[index] = Math.max(
					track.min,
					Math.min(track.max, contribution),
				);
			}
		}
	}

	const totalBaseSize =
		sizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) + gap * (trackCount - 1);
	const remaining = hasExplicitSize ? availableSize - totalBaseSize : 0;

	const totalFr = normalizedTracks.reduce((sum, track) => {
		if (track.kind === 'fr') {
			return sum + track.value;
		}

		if (track.kind === 'minmax-fr') {
			return sum + track.fr;
		}

		return sum;
	}, 0);

	if (totalFr > 0 && remaining > 0) {
		for (let index = 0; index < normalizedTracks.length; index++) {
			const track = normalizedTracks[index]!;
			const fr =
				track.kind === 'fr'
					? track.value
					: track.kind === 'minmax-fr'
						? track.fr
						: 0;

			if (fr > 0) {
				sizes[index] = sizes[index]! + remaining * (fr / totalFr);
			}
		}
	}

	return sizes;
};

const layoutGridContainer = (container: DOMElement): void => {
	const yoga = container.yogaNode;
	const style = container.style;

	if (!yoga) {
		return;
	}

	const items: GridItem[] = container.childNodes
		.filter(isDOMElement)
		.filter(child => Boolean(child.yogaNode))
		.filter(child => child.yogaNode!.getDisplay() !== Yoga.DISPLAY_NONE)
		.map(child => ({
			child,
			column: parseGridLine(child.style.gridColumn),
			row: parseGridLine(child.style.gridRow),
			contentWidth: child.yogaNode!.getComputedWidth(),
			contentHeight: child.yogaNode!.getComputedHeight(),
			marginLeft: getMargin(child.style, 'left'),
			marginRight: getMargin(child.style, 'right'),
			marginTop: getMargin(child.style, 'top'),
			marginBottom: getMargin(child.style, 'bottom'),
			columnStart: 0,
			columnEnd: 0,
			rowStart: 0,
			rowEnd: 0,
		}));

	if (items.length === 0) {
		return;
	}

	const columnsTemplate = parseTrackList(style.gridTemplateColumns);
	const rowsTemplate = parseTrackList(style.gridTemplateRows);

	const initialColumnCount = Math.max(1, columnsTemplate.length);
	const {items: placedItems, columnCount, rowCount} = determinePlacements(
		items,
		initialColumnCount,
	);

	const borderLeft = yoga.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
	const borderRight = yoga.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
	const borderTop = yoga.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const borderBottom = yoga.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);
	const paddingLeft = yoga.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
	const paddingRight = yoga.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
	const paddingTop = yoga.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const paddingBottom = yoga.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);

	const columnGap = style.columnGap ?? style.gap ?? 0;
	const rowGap = style.rowGap ?? style.gap ?? 0;

	const hasExplicitWidth = 'width' in style;
	const hasExplicitHeight = 'height' in style;

	const containerWidth = Math.max(
		0,
		yoga.getComputedWidth() - borderLeft - borderRight - paddingLeft - paddingRight,
	);
	const containerHeight = Math.max(
		0,
		yoga.getComputedHeight() - borderTop - borderBottom - paddingTop - paddingBottom,
	);

	const columnSizes = resolveTrackSizes(
		columnsTemplate,
		placedItems,
		columnCount,
		'column',
		columnGap,
		containerWidth,
		hasExplicitWidth,
	);

	const rowSizes = resolveTrackSizes(
		rowsTemplate,
		placedItems,
		rowCount,
		'row',
		rowGap,
		containerHeight,
		hasExplicitHeight,
	);

	const totalColumnSize =
		columnSizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) +
		columnGap * (columnCount - 1);
	const totalRowSize =
		rowSizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) + rowGap * (rowCount - 1);

	if (!hasExplicitWidth) {
		yoga.setWidth(totalColumnSize + borderLeft + borderRight + paddingLeft + paddingRight);
	}

	if (!hasExplicitHeight) {
		yoga.setHeight(totalRowSize + borderTop + borderBottom + paddingTop + paddingBottom);
	}

	const columnOffsets: number[] = [0];
	for (let index = 0; index < columnCount; index++) {
		columnOffsets.push(
			columnOffsets[index]! + columnSizes[index]! + (index < columnCount - 1 ? columnGap : 0),
		);
	}

	const rowOffsets: number[] = [0];
	for (let index = 0; index < rowCount; index++) {
		rowOffsets.push(
			rowOffsets[index]! + rowSizes[index]! + (index < rowCount - 1 ? rowGap : 0),
		);
	}

	for (const item of placedItems) {
		const childYoga = item.child.yogaNode!;
		const width =
			columnOffsets[item.columnEnd]! -
			columnOffsets[item.columnStart]! -
			(item.columnEnd - item.columnStart - 1) * columnGap;
		const height =
			rowOffsets[item.rowEnd]! -
			rowOffsets[item.rowStart]! -
			(item.rowEnd - item.rowStart - 1) * rowGap;

		childYoga.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE);
		childYoga.setPosition(
			Yoga.EDGE_LEFT,
			paddingLeft + columnOffsets[item.columnStart]!,
		);
		childYoga.setPosition(
			Yoga.EDGE_TOP,
			paddingTop + rowOffsets[item.rowStart]!,
		);
		childYoga.setWidth(Math.max(0, width - item.marginLeft - item.marginRight));
		childYoga.setHeight(Math.max(0, height - item.marginTop - item.marginBottom));
	}
};

const computeGridLayouts = (node: DOMElement): void => {
	if (isGridContainer(node)) {
		layoutGridContainer(node);
	}

	for (const child of node.childNodes) {
		if (isDOMElement(child) && child.yogaNode) {
			computeGridLayouts(child);
		}
	}
};

export const applyGridLayout = (rootNode: DOMElement): void => {
	const rootYoga = rootNode.yogaNode;

	if (!rootYoga) {
		return;
	}

	// Ensure grid children are measured at their natural content size before
	// grid tracks are resolved.
	for (const container of collectGridContainers(rootNode)) {
		const yoga = container.yogaNode!;

		for (let index = 0; index < yoga.getChildCount(); index++) {
			const child = yoga.getChild(index);
			child.setFlexShrink(0);
			child.setFlexGrow(0);
		}
	}

	rootYoga.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);

	// Apply the grid layout and recalculate so explicitly positioned children
	// stay in place and auto-sized containers get their final dimensions.
	computeGridLayouts(rootNode);
	rootYoga.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
	computeGridLayouts(rootNode);
	rootYoga.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
};

export default applyGridLayout;

OUTPUT:
   100			() => {},
   101			() => {},
   102			() => {},
   103		);
   104	
   105		let teardownSucceeded = false;
   106	
   107		try {
   108			// Synchronously render the React tree into the container
   109			reconciler.updateContainerSync(node, container, null, () => {});
   110			reconciler.flushSyncWork();
   111	
   112			// Yoga layout has already been calculated by onComputeLayout during commit.
   113			// Render the DOM tree to a string — this captures the dynamic (non-static) output.
   114			const {output} = renderer(rootNode, false);
   115	
   116			// Tear down: unmount the tree so the reconciler cleans up child nodes
   117			// and runs effect cleanup functions. Child Yoga nodes are freed by the
   118			// reconciler's removeChildFromContainer → cleanupYogaNode → freeRecursive.
   119			reconciler.updateContainerSync(null, container, null, () => {});
   120			reconciler.flushSyncWork();
   121			teardownSucceeded = true;
   122	
   123			// Free the root yoga node itself (children already freed by reconciler)
   124			rootNode.yogaNode!.free();
   125	
   126			// Re-throw after full cleanup so callers see the original error.
   127			if (uncaughtError !== undefined) {
   128				throw uncaughtError instanceof Error
   129					? uncaughtError
   130					: // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-base-to-string
   131						new Error(String(uncaughtError));
   132			}
   133	
   134			// The renderer appends a trailing newline to static output for terminal
   135			// rendering (so dynamic output starts on a fresh line). Strip it here
   136			// so renderToString returns clean output.
   137			const normalizedStaticOutput = capturedStaticOutput.endsWith('\n')
   138				? capturedStaticOutput.slice(0, -1)
   139				: capturedStaticOutput;
   140	
   141			if (normalizedStaticOutput && output) {
   142				return normalizedStaticOutput + '\n' + output;
   143			}
   144	
   145			return normalizedStaticOutput || output;
   146		} finally {
   147			// Ensure native Yoga memory is freed even if rendering or teardown threw.
   148			// Yoga nodes are WASM-backed and not garbage collected.
   149			if (!teardownSucceeded && rootNode.yogaNode) {
   150				try {
   151					// If reconciler teardown failed, some child nodes may not have been
   152					// freed. Use freeRecursive to clean up the entire tree as best-effort.
   153					rootNode.yogaNode.freeRecursive();
   154				} catch {
   155					// Best-effort: node may already be partially freed
   156				}
   157			}
   158		}
   159	};
   160	
   161	export default renderToString;

cd /app && cat > grid-scenarios.tsx <<'EOF'
import React from 'react';
import {renderToString, Box, Text} from './src/index.js';

const show = (name: string, node: React.ReactNode) => {
	console.log(`--- ${name} ---`);
	console.log(JSON.stringify(renderToString(node as React.ReactElement)));
};

// 1. Fixed columns
show('fixed columns + gap', <Box width={11} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" columnGap={1}>
	<Text>A</Text><Text>B</Text>
</Box>);

// 2. fr columns
show('fr columns 1fr 2fr', <Box width={9} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="1fr 2fr" columnGap={1}>
	<Text>A</Text><Text>B</Text>
</Box>);

// 3. auto columns
show('auto columns', <Box width={10} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="auto auto" columnGap={1}>
	<Text>A</Text><Text>B</Text>
</Box>);

// 4. minmax fixed max
show('minmax fixed max', <Box width={20} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="minmax(3, 5) minmax(3, 5)" columnGap={1}>
	<Text>A</Text><Text>B</Text>
</Box>);

// 5. minmax fr max
show('minmax fr max', <Box width={20} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="minmax(3, 1fr) minmax(3, 2fr)" columnGap={1}>
	<Text>A</Text><Text>B</Text>
</Box>);

// 6. explicit rows + rowGap
show('explicit rows + rowGap', <Box width={5} height={5} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5" gridTemplateRows="2 2" rowGap={1}>
	<Text>A</Text><Text>B</Text>
</Box>);

// 7. auto rows
show('auto rows', <Box width={5} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5">
	<Text>A</Text><Text>B</Text><Text>C</Text>
</Box>);

// 8. explicit gridColumn single
show('gridColumn single', <Box width={15} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5 5" columnGap={1}>
	<Text gridColumn={3}>C</Text>
</Box>);

// 9. explicit gridRow span
show('gridRow span', <Box width={5} height={5} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5" gridTemplateRows="1 1 1" rowGap={1}>
	<Text gridRow="1 / 3">A</Text>
</Box>);

// 10. explicit gridColumn span
show('gridColumn span', <Box width={15} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5 5" columnGap={1}>
	<Text gridColumn="1 / 3">A</Text>
</Box>);
EOF

   541	let callCount = 0;
   542	export const applyGridLayout = (rootNode: DOMElement): void => {
   543		callCount++;
   544		console.error('applyGridLayout call', callCount);
   545		const rootYoga = rootNode.yogaNode;
   546	
   547		if (!rootYoga) {
   548			return;
   549		}
   550	
   551		// Ensure grid children are measured at their natural content size before
   552		// grid tracks are resolved.
   553		for (const container of collectGridContainers(rootNode)) {
   554			const yoga = container.yogaNode!;
   555	
   556			for (let index = 0; index < yoga.getChildCount(); index++) {
   557				const child = yoga.getChild(index);
   558				child.setFlexShrink(0);
   559				child.setFlexGrow(0);
   560				console.error('set flex child', index);
   561			}
   562		}
   563	
   564		rootYoga.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
   565	
   566		// Apply the grid layout and recalculate so explicitly positioned children

COMMAND:
cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p = Path('src/grid.ts')
s = p.read_text()

# Fix parseTrackList to split outside parentheses
old = """const parseTrackList = (value?: string): Track[] => {
	if (!value) {
		return [];
	}

	return value
		.trim()
		.split(/\\s+/)
		.map(parseTrack)
		.filter((track): track is Track => Boolean(track));
};"""
new = """const splitTrackList = (value: string): string[] => {
	const tokens: string[] = [];
	let current = '';
	let depth = 0;

	for (const character of value.trim()) {
		if (character === '(') {
			depth++;
		} else if (character === ')') {
			depth--;
		}

		if (/\\s/.test(character) && depth === 0) {
			if (current) {
				tokens.push(current);
				current = '';
			}
		} else {
			current += character;
		}
	}

	if (current) {
		tokens.push(current);
	}

	return tokens;
};

const parseTrackList = (value?: string): Track[] => {
	if (!value) {
		return [];
	}

	return splitTrackList(value)
		.map(parseTrack)
		.filter((track): track is Track => Boolean(track));
};"""
if old not in s:
	print("old parseTrackList not found")
else:
	s = s.replace(old, new)

# Remove leftover debug
s = s.replace("""\t\t\t\tchild.setFlexShrink(0);
\t\t\t\tchild.setFlexGrow(0);
\t\t\t\tconsole.error('set flex child', index);""", """\t\t\t\tchild.setFlexShrink(0);
\t\t\t\tchild.setFlexGrow(0);""")
s = s.replace("""\t\t\t\tconst child = yoga.getChild(index);
\t\t\t\tchild.setFlexShrink(0);
\t\t\t\tchild.setFlexGrow(0);
\t\t\t\tconsole.error('set flex child', index);""", """\t\t\t\tconst child = yoga.getChild(index);
\t\t\t\tchild.setFlexShrink(0);
\t\t\t\tchild.setFlexGrow(0);""")

p.write_text(s)
print("done")

COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > test/grid.tsx <<'EOF'
import React from 'react';
import test from 'ava';
import {Box, Text} from '../src/index.js';
import {
	renderToString,
	renderToStringAsync,
} from './helpers/render-to-string.js';

test('display grid places children into fixed columns', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box width={11} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" columnGap={1}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A     B');
});

test('grid distributes space proportionally between fr columns', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box width={10} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="1fr 2fr" columnGap={1}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A  B');
});

test('grid supports auto columns', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box width={10} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="auto auto" columnGap={1}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A  B');
});

test('grid supports minmax with fixed maximum', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box
			width={20}
			display="grid"
			gridTemplateColumns="minmax(3, 5) minmax(3, 5)"
			columnGap={1}
		>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A    B');
});

test('grid supports minmax with fr maximum', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box
			width={20}
			display="grid"
			gridTemplateColumns="minmax(3, 1fr) minmax(3, 2fr)"
			columnGap={1}
		>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A       B');
});

test('grid supports explicit rows and row gap', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box
			width={5}
			height={5}
			display="grid"
			gridTemplateColumns="5"
			gridTemplateRows="2 2"
			rowGap={1}
		>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A\n\n\nB\n');
});

test('grid creates rows automatically when gridTemplateRows is omitted', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box width={5} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5">
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A\nB\nC');
});

test('grid accepts a single index for gridColumn', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box width={15} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5 5" columnGap={1}>
			<Text gridColumn={3}>C</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'C');
});

test('grid accepts start / end for gridColumn', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box width={15} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5 5" columnGap={1}>
			<Text gridColumn="1 / 3">A</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A');
});

test('grid accepts start / end for gridRow', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box
			width={5}
			height={5}
			display="grid"
			gridTemplateColumns="5"
			gridTemplateRows="1 1 1"
			rowGap={1}
		>
			<Text gridRow="1 / 3">A</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A\n\n\n\n');
});

test('grid gap shorthand applies to tracks', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box width={11} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" gap={1}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A     B');
});

test('grid with fr rows distributes remaining height', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box
			width={5}
			height={5}
			display="grid"
			gridTemplateColumns="5"
			gridTemplateRows="1fr 2fr"
			rowGap={1}
		>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A\n\nB\n\n');
});

test('grid display grid - concurrent', async t => {
	const output = await renderToStringAsync(
		<Box width={11} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" columnGap={1}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A     B');
});

import React from 'react';
import {renderToString, Box, Text} from './src/index.js';

const show = (name: string, node: React.ReactNode) => {
	console.log(`--- ${name} ---`);
	console.log(JSON.stringify(renderToString(node as React.ReactElement)));
};

// Auto container width with fixed columns
show('auto width fixed cols', <Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="3 3" columnGap={1}>
	<Text>A</Text><Text>B</Text>
</Box>);

// Mixed explicit + auto placement
show('mixed placement', <Box width={15} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5 5" columnGap={1}>
	<Text gridColumn={3}>C</Text>
	<Text>A</Text>
	<Text>B</Text>
</Box>);

// Span with gap
show('span with gap', <Box width={15} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5 5" columnGap={1}>
	<Text gridColumn="1 / 3">A</Text><Text>B</Text>
</Box>);

// Padding on grid container
show('grid padding', <Box width={13} padding={1} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" columnGap={1}>
	<Text>A</Text><Text>B</Text>
</Box>);

// Border on grid container
show('grid border', <Box width={13} borderStyle="round" display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" columnGap={1}>
	<Text>A</Text><Text>B</Text>
</Box>);

// auto rows with explicit gridRow
show('auto rows explicit row', <Box width={5} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5">
	<Text gridRow={3}>C</Text>
	<Text>A</Text>
	<Text>B</Text>
</Box>);

// fr rows omitted -> auto
show('fr rows no height', <Box width={5} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5" gridTemplateRows="1fr 1fr">
	<Text>A</Text><Text>B</Text>
</Box>);
EOF

    36	const parseTrack = (token: string): Track | undefined => {
    37		const value = token.trim();
    38	
    39		if (value === 'auto') {
    40			return {kind: 'auto'};
    41		}
    42	
    43		if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(value)) {
    44			return {kind: 'fixed', value: Number(value)};
    45		}
    46	
    47		if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?fr$/.test(value)) {
    48			return {kind: 'fr', value: Number.parseFloat(value)};
    49		}
    50	
    51		if (value.startsWith('minmax(') && value.endsWith(')')) {
    52			const [minValue, maxValue] = value
    53				.slice('minmax('.length, -1)
    54				.split(',')
    55				.map(part => part.trim());
    56	
    57			if (!minValue || !maxValue) {
    58				return undefined;
    59			}
    60	
    61			const min = Number(minValue);
    62	
    63			if (maxValue.endsWith('fr')) {
    64				return {kind: 'minmax-fr', min, fr: Number.parseFloat(maxValue)};
    65			}
    66	
    67			return {kind: 'minmax', min, max: Number(maxValue)};
    68		}
    69	
    70		return undefined;
    71	};
    72	
    73	const splitTrackList = (value: string): string[] => {
    74		const tokens: string[] = [];
    75		let current = '';
    76		let depth = 0;
    77	
    78		for (const character of value.trim()) {
    79			if (character === '(') {
    80				depth++;
    81			} else if (character === ')') {
    82				depth--;
    83			}
    84	
    85			if (/\s/.test(character) && depth === 0) {
    86				if (current) {
    87					tokens.push(current);
    88					current = '';
    89				}
    90			} else {
    91				current += character;
    92			}
    93		}
    94	
    95		if (current) {
    96			tokens.push(current);
    97		}
    98	
    99		return tokens;
   100	};
   101

   103		if (!value) {
   104			return [];
   105		}
   106	
   107		return splitTrackList(value).map(parseTrack).filter(Boolean);
   108	};

from pathlib import Path
p = Path('src/grid.ts')
lines = p.read_text().split('\n')

# Find and modify lines
out = []
i = 0
while i < len(lines):
    line = lines[i]
    # parseTrackList filter
    if line.strip() == ".filter((track): track is Track => Boolean(track));":
        out.append("\t\t.filter((track): track is Track => track !== undefined);")

    36	const parseTrack = (token: string): Track | undefined => {
    37		const value = token.trim();
    38	
    39		if (value === 'auto') {
    40			return {kind: 'auto'};
    41		}
    42	
    43		if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(value)) {
    44			return {kind: 'fixed', value: Number(value)};
    45		}
    46	
    47		if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?fr$/.test(value)) {
    48			return {kind: 'fr', value: Number.parseFloat(value)};
    49		}
    50	
    51		if (value.startsWith('minmax(') && value.endsWith(')')) {
    52			const [minValue, maxValue] = value
    53				.slice('minmax('.length, -1)
    54				.split(',')
    55				.map(part => part.trim());
    56	
    57			if (!minValue || !maxValue) {
    58				return undefined;
    59			}
    60	
    61			const min = Number(minValue);
    62	
    63			if (maxValue.endsWith('fr')) {
    64				return {kind: 'minmax-fr', min, fr: Number.parseFloat(maxValue)};
    65			}
    66	
    67			return {kind: 'minmax', min, max: Number(maxValue)};
    68		}
    69	
    70		return undefined;
    71	};

    73	const splitTrackList = (value: string): string[] => {
    74		const tokens: string[] = [];
    75		let current = '';
    76		let depth = 0;
    77	
    78		for (const character of value.trim()) {
    79			if (character === '(') {
    80				depth++;
    81			} else if (character === ')') {
    82				depth--;
    83			}
    84	
    85			if (/\s/.test(character) && depth === 0) {
    86				if (current) {
    87					tokens.push(current);
    88					current = '';
    89				}
    90			} else {
    91				current += character;
    92			}
    93		}
    94	
    95		if (current) {
    96			tokens.push(current);
    97		}
    98	
    99		return tokens;
   100	};

   104			return [];
   105		}
   106

   114			return undefined;
   115		}

   117		if (typeof value === 'number') {
   118			return {start: value, end: value + 1};
   119		}

   122

   124			const start = Number(parts[0]);
   125			return {start, end: start + 1};
   126		}
   127	
   128		return {start: Number(parts[0]), end: Number(parts[1])};
   129	};

   247				columnEnd = 1;
   248	
   249				while (isOccupied(occupied, rowStart, rowEnd, columnStart, columnEnd)) {
   250					columnStart++;
   251					columnEnd++;
   252	
   253					if (columnStart >= columnCount) {
   254						columnStart = 0;
   255						columnEnd = 1;
   256						rowStart++;
   257						rowEnd++;
   258					}
   259				}
   260			} else {
   261				columnStart = cursorColumn;
   262				columnEnd = cursorColumn + 1;
   263	
   264				while (columnEnd > columnCount) {
   265					cursorRow++;
   266					cursorColumn = 0;
   267					columnStart = 0;
   268					columnEnd = 1;
   269				}
   270	
   271				while (
   272					isOccupied(occupied, cursorRow, cursorRow + 1, columnStart, columnEnd)
   273				) {
   274					cursorColumn++;
   275					columnStart++;
   276					columnEnd++;
   277	
   278					if (columnEnd > columnCount) {
   279						cursorRow++;
   280						cursorColumn = 0;
   281						columnStart = 0;
   282						columnEnd = 1;
   283					}
   284				}
   285	
   286				rowStart = cursorRow;
   287				rowEnd = cursorRow + 1;
   288			}
   289	
   290			item.columnStart = columnStart;
   291			item.columnEnd = columnEnd;
   292			item.rowStart = rowStart;
   293			item.rowEnd = rowEnd;
   294	
   295			occupy(occupied, rowStart, rowEnd, columnStart, columnEnd);
   296	
   297			if (!column) {
   298				cursorColumn = columnEnd;
   299				if (cursorColumn >= columnCount) {
   300					cursorRow++;
   301					cursorColumn = 0;
   302				}
   303			}
   304		}
   305	
   306		let rowCount = 0;
   307	
   308		for (const item of items) {
   309			rowCount = Math.max(rowCount, item.rowEnd);
   310			columnCount = Math.max(columnCount, item.columnEnd);
   311		}
   312	
   313		return {items, columnCount, rowCount};
   314	};
   315	
   316	const resolveTrackSizes = (
   317		tracks: Track[],
   318		items: GridItem[],
   319		trackCount: number,
   320		axis: 'column' | 'row',
   321		gap: number,
   322		availableSize: number,
   323		hasExplicitSize: boolean,
   324	): number[] => {
   325		const normalizedTracks: Track[] = [];
   326	
   327		for (let index = 0; index < trackCount; index++) {
   328			let track = tracks[index] ?? {kind: 'auto'};
   329	
   330			// `fr` tracks can't resolve against an auto-sized container.
   331			if (!hasExplicitSize && track.kind === 'fr') {
   332				track = {kind: 'auto'};
   333			}
   334	
   335			if (!hasExplicitSize && track.kind === 'minmax-fr') {
   336				track = {kind: 'minmax', min: track.min, max: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY};
   337			}
   338	
   339			normalizedTracks.push(track);
   340		}
   341	
   342		const sizes: number[] = normalizedTracks.map(track => {
   343			switch (track.kind) {
   344				case 'fixed': {
   345					return track.value;
   346				}
   347	
   348				case 'auto': {
   349					return 0;
   350				}
   351	
   352				case 'fr': {
   353					return 0;
   354				}
   355	
   356				case 'minmax': {
   357					return track.min;
   358				}
   359	
   360				case 'minmax-fr': {
   361					return track.min;
   362				}
   363			}
   364	
   365			return 0;
   366		});
   367	
   368		for (const item of items) {
   369			const start = axis === 'column' ? item.columnStart : item.rowStart;
   370			const end = axis === 'column' ? item.columnEnd : item.rowEnd;
   371			const span = Math.max(1, end - start);
   372			const contentSize =
   373				(axis === 'column' ? item.contentWidth : item.contentHeight) +
   374				(axis === 'column'
   375					? item.marginLeft + item.marginRight
   376					: item.marginTop + item.marginBottom);
   377	
   378			const contribution = contentSize / span;
   379	
   380			for (let index = start; index < end; index++) {
   381				const track = normalizedTracks[index]!;
   382	
   383				if (track.kind === 'auto') {
   384					sizes[index] = Math.max(sizes[index]!, contribution);
   385				}
   386	
   387				if (track.kind === 'minmax') {
   388					sizes[index] = Math.max(track.min, Math.min(track.max, contribution));
   389				}
   390			}
   391		}
   392	
   393		let totalBaseSize = gap * (trackCount - 1);
   394	
   395		for (const size of sizes) {
   396			totalBaseSize += size;
   397		}
   398	
   399		const remaining = hasExplicitSize ? availableSize - totalBaseSize : 0;
   400	
   401		let totalFr = 0;
   402	
   403		for (const track of normalizedTracks) {
   404			if (track.kind === 'fr') {
   405				totalFr += track.value;
   406			} else if (track.kind === 'minmax-fr') {
   407				totalFr += track.fr;
   408			}
   409		}
   410	
   411		if (totalFr > 0 && remaining > 0) {
   412			for (const [index, normalizedTrack] of normalizedTracks.entries()) {
   413				const track = normalizedTrack;
   414				const fr =
   415					track.kind === 'fr'
   416						? track.value
   417						: track.kind === 'minmax-fr'
   418							? track.fr
   419							: 0;
   420	
   421				if (fr > 0) {
   422					sizes[index] = sizes[index]! + remaining * (fr / totalFr);
   423				}
   424			}
   425		}
   426	
   427		return sizes;
   428	};
   429	
   430	const layoutGridContainer = (container: DOMElement): void => {

   747					(!this.hasPendingThrottledRender && this.fullStaticOutput === '');

   753			}

     1	import EventEmitter from 'node:events';
     2	import {spy} from 'sinon';
     3	
     4	// Fake process.stdout
     5	export type FakeStdout = {
     6		get: () => string;
     7		getWrites: () => string[];
     8	} & NodeJS.WriteStream;
     9	
    10	const createStdout = (columns?: number, isTTY?: boolean): FakeStdout => {
    11		const stdout = new EventEmitter() as unknown as FakeStdout;
    12		stdout.columns = columns ?? 100;
    13		stdout.isTTY = isTTY ?? true;
    14	
    15		const write = spy();
    16		stdout.write = write;
    17	
    18		stdout.get = () => write.lastCall.args[0] as string;
    19	
    20		stdout.getWrites = () => (write.args as string[][]).map(args => args[0]!);
    21	
    22		return stdout;
    23	};
    24	
    25	export default createStdout;

    65				// interactive output will override last line of static output
    66				staticOutput: staticOutput ? `${staticOutput.get().output}\n` : '',
    67			};
    68		}
    69	
    70		return {
    71			output: '',
    72			outputHeight: 0,
    73			staticOutput: '',
    74		};
    75	};
    76	
    77	export default renderer;

from pathlib import Path
p = Path('src/grid.ts')
s = p.read_text()
start = s.find('export const applyGridLayout')

else:
	new = """export const applyGridLayout = (rootNode: DOMElement): void => {
	const rootYoga = rootNode.yogaNode;

	if (!rootYoga) {
		return;
	}

	// Ensure grid children are measured at their natural content size before
	// grid tracks are resolved. Reset any explicit placement from a previous
	// layout so auto tracks don't grow on subsequent renders.
	for (const container of collectGridContainers(rootNode)) {
		const children = container.childNodes
			.filter(isDomElement)
			.filter(child => Boolean(child.yogaNode));

		for (const child of children) {
			const childYoga = child.yogaNode!;
			childYoga.setFlexShrink(0);
			childYoga.setFlexGrow(0);
			childYoga.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_RELATIVE);
			childYoga.setPosition(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT, 0);
			childYoga.setPosition(Yoga.EDGE_TOP, 0);

			if (typeof child.style.width === 'number') {
				childYoga.setWidth(child.style.width);
			} else {
				childYoga.setWidthAuto();
			}

			if (typeof child.style.height === 'number') {
				childYoga.setHeight(child.style.height);
			} else {
				childYoga.setHeightAuto();
			}
		}
	}

	rootYoga.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);

	// Apply the grid layout and recalculate so explicitly positioned children
	// stay in place and auto-sized containers get their final dimensions.
	computeGridLayouts(rootNode);
	rootYoga.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
	computeGridLayouts(rootNode);
	rootYoga.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
};

	p.write_text(s)

import React from 'react';
import {renderToString, Box, Text} from './src/index.js';

const show = (name, node) => console.log(name, JSON.stringify(renderToString(node)));

// 4 children, 3 columns -> 4th wraps to second row
show('wrap rows', <Box width={15} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5 5" columnGap={1} rowGap={1}>
	<Box><Text>A</Text></Box><Box><Text>B</Text></Box><Box><Text>C</Text></Box><Box><Text>D</Text></Box>
</Box>);

// gridRow explicit with Box, auto rows
show('auto rows explicit row', <Box width={5} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5">
	<Box gridRow={3}><Text>C</Text></Box>
	<Box><Text>A</Text></Box>
	<Box><Text>B</Text></Box>
</Box>);

// gridColumn and gridRow together
show('col+row', <Box width={15} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5 5" gridTemplateRows="2 2" columnGap={1} rowGap={1}>
	<Box gridColumn={2} gridRow={2}><Text>X</Text></Box>
</Box>);

// no gridTemplateColumns -> one column
show('no columns template', <Box display="grid">
	<Box><Text>A</Text></Box><Box><Text>B</Text></Box>
</Box>);

// fr columns auto width -> auto
show('fr auto width', <Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="1fr 1fr" columnGap={1}>
	<Box><Text>A</Text></Box><Box><Text>B</Text></Box>
</Box>);

// minmax fr rows
show('minmax fr rows', <Box width={5} height={5} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5" gridTemplateRows="minmax(1, 1fr) minmax(2, 2fr)" rowGap={1}>
	<Box><Text>A</Text></Box><Box><Text>B</Text></Box>
</Box>);
EOF

STEP 188 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat >> test/grid.tsx <<'EOF'

test('grid auto-places items around explicit rows', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box width={5} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5">
			<Box gridRow={3}><Text>C</Text></Box>
			<Box><Text>A</Text></Box>
			<Box><Text>B</Text></Box>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A\nB\nC');
});

test('grid wraps auto-placed items to new rows', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box
			width={15}
			display="grid"
			gridTemplateColumns="5 5 5"
			columnGap={1}
			rowGap={1}
		>
			<Box><Text>A</Text></Box>
			<Box><Text>B</Text></Box>
			<Box><Text>C</Text></Box>
			<Box><Text>D</Text></Box>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A     B     C\n\nD');
});

test('grid layout is stable across rerenders', t => {
	const stdout = createStdout(100);
	const {rerender} = render(
		<Box width={10} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="auto auto" columnGap={1}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
		{stdout, debug: true, exitOnCtrlC: false, patchConsole: false},
	);

	t.is(stdout.get(), 'A B');

	rerender(
		<Box width={10} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="auto auto" columnGap={1}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(stdout.get(), 'A B');

	rerender(
		<Box width={10} display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="auto auto" columnGap={1}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(stdout.get(), 'A B');
});
EOF

    52		}
    53	
    54		if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?fr$/.test(value)) {
    55			return {kind: 'fr', value: Number.parseFloat(value)};
    56		}
    57	
    58		if (value.startsWith('minmax(') && value.endsWith(')')) {
    59			const [minValue, maxValue] = value
    60				.slice('minmax('.length, -1)
    61				.split(',')
    62				.map(part => part.trim());
    63	
    64			if (!minValue || !maxValue) {
    65				return undefined;
    66			}
    67	
    68			const min = Number(minValue);
    69	
    70			if (maxValue.endsWith('fr')) {
    71				return {kind: 'minmax-fr', min, fr: Number.parseFloat(maxValue)};
    72			}
    73	
    74			return {kind: 'minmax', min, max: Number(maxValue)};
    75		}
    76	
    77		return undefined;
    78	};
    79	
    80	const splitTrackList = (value: string): string[] => {
    81		const tokens: string[] = [];
    82		let current = '';
    83		let depth = 0;
    84	
    85		for (const character of value.trim()) {
    86			if (character === '(') {
    87				depth++;
    88			} else if (character === ')') {
    89				depth--;
    90			}
    91	
    92			if (/\s/.test(character) && depth === 0) {
    93				if (current) {
    94					tokens.push(current);
    95					current = '';
    96				}
    97			} else {
    98				current += character;
    99			}
   100		}
   101	
   102		if (current) {
   103			tokens.push(current);
   104		}
   105	
   106		return tokens;
   107	};
   108	
   109	const parseTrackList = (value?: string): Track[] => {
   110		if (!value) {
   111			return [];
   112		}
   113	
   114		return splitTrackList(value)
   115			.map(track => parseTrack(track))
   116			.filter((track): track is Track => track !== undefined);
   117	};
   118	
   119	const parseGridLine = (value?: number | string): GridPlacement | undefined => {
   120		if (value === undefined) {
   121			return undefined;
   122		}
   123	
   124		if (typeof value === 'number') {
   125			return {start: value, end: value + 1};
   126		}
   127	
   128		const parts = value.split('/').map(part => part.trim());
   129	
   130		if (parts.length === 1) {
   131			const start = Number(parts[0]);
   132			return {start, end: start + 1};
   133		}
   134	
   135		return {start: Number(parts[0]), end: Number(parts[1])};
   136	};
   137	
   138	const getMargin = (
   139		style: Styles,
   140		axis: 'left' | 'right' | 'top' | 'bottom',
   141	): number => {
   142		const margin = style.margin ?? 0;
   143	
   144		switch (axis) {
   145			case 'left': {
   146				return style.marginLeft ?? style.marginX ?? margin;
   147			}
   148	
   149			case 'right': {
   150				return style.marginRight ?? style.marginX ?? margin;
   151			}
   152	
   153			case 'top': {
   154				return style.marginTop ?? style.marginY ?? margin;
   155			}
   156	
   157			case 'bottom': {
   158				return style.marginBottom ?? style.marginY ?? margin;
   159			}
   160		}
   161	};
   162	
   163	const isGridContainer = (node: DOMElement): boolean =>
   164		Boolean(node.yogaNode) && node.style.display === 'grid';
   165	
   166	const collectGridContainers = (
   167		node: DOMElement,
   168		containers: DOMElement[] = [],
   169	): DOMElement[] => {
   170		if (isGridContainer(node)) {
   171			containers.push(node);
   172		}
   173	
   174		for (const child of node.childNodes) {
   175			if (isDomElement(child) && child.yogaNode) {
   176				collectGridContainers(child, containers);
   177			}
   178		}
   179	
   180		return containers;
