   180		return containers;
   181	};
   182	
   183	const isOccupied = (occupied: Set<string>, range: GridRange): boolean => {
   184		for (let row = range.rowStart; row < range.rowEnd; row++) {
   185			for (let column = range.columnStart; column < range.columnEnd; column++) {
   186				if (occupied.has(`${row}:${column}`)) {
   187					return true;
   188				}
   189			}
   190		}
   191	
   192		return false;
   193	};
   194	
   195	const occupy = (occupied: Set<string>, range: GridRange): void => {
   196		for (let row = range.rowStart; row < range.rowEnd; row++) {
   197			for (let column = range.columnStart; column < range.columnEnd; column++) {
   198				occupied.add(`${row}:${column}`);
   199			}
   200		}
   201	};
   202	
   203	const determinePlacements = (
   204		items: GridItem[],
   205		initialColumnCount: number,
   206	): {items: GridItem[]; columnCount: number; rowCount: number} => {
   207		const occupied = new Set<string>();
   208		let columnCount = initialColumnCount;
   209		let cursorRow = 0;
   210		let cursorColumn = 0;
   211	
   212		for (const item of items) {
   213			const {column, row} = item;
   214			let columnStart = 0;
   215			let columnEnd = 0;
   216			let rowStart = 0;
   217			let rowEnd = 0;
   218	
   219			if (column) {
   220				columnStart = column.start - 1;
   221				columnEnd = column.end - 1;
   222				columnCount = Math.max(columnCount, columnEnd);
   223			}
   224	
   225			if (row) {
   226				rowStart = row.start - 1;
   227				rowEnd = row.end - 1;
   228			}
   229	
   230			if (column && row) {
   231				// Explicit placement.
   232			} else if (column) {
   233				rowStart = 0;
   234				rowEnd = 1;
   235	
   236				while (isOccupied(occupied, {rowStart, rowEnd, columnStart, columnEnd})) {
   237					rowStart++;
   238					rowEnd++;
   239				}
   240			} else if (row) {
   241				columnStart = 0;
   242				columnEnd = 1;
   243	
   244				while (isOccupied(occupied, {rowStart, rowEnd, columnStart, columnEnd})) {
   245					columnStart++;
   246					columnEnd++;
   247	
   248					if (columnStart >= columnCount) {
   249						columnStart = 0;
   250						columnEnd = 1;
   251						rowStart++;
   252						rowEnd++;
   253					}
   254				}
   255			} else {
   256				columnStart = cursorColumn;
   257				columnEnd = cursorColumn + 1;
   258	
   259				while (columnEnd > columnCount) {
   260					cursorRow++;
   261					cursorColumn = 0;
   262					columnStart = 0;
   263					columnEnd = 1;
   264				}
   265	
   266				while (
   267					isOccupied(occupied, {
   268						rowStart: cursorRow,
   269						rowEnd: cursorRow + 1,
   270						columnStart,
   271						columnEnd,
   272					})
   273				) {
   274					cursorColumn++;
   275					columnStart++;
   276					columnEnd++;
   277	
   278					if (columnEnd > columnCount) {
   279						cursorRow++;
   280						cursorColumn = 0;
   281						columnStart = 0;
   282						columnEnd = 1;
   283					}
   284				}
   285	
   286				rowStart = cursorRow;
   287				rowEnd = cursorRow + 1;
   288			}
   289	
   290			item.columnStart = columnStart;
   291			item.columnEnd = columnEnd;
   292			item.rowStart = rowStart;
   293			item.rowEnd = rowEnd;
   294	
   295			occupy(occupied, {rowStart, rowEnd, columnStart, columnEnd});
   296	
   297			if (!column && !row) {
   298				cursorColumn = columnEnd;
   299				if (cursorColumn >= columnCount) {
   300					cursorRow++;
   301					cursorColumn = 0;
   302				}
   303			}
   304		}
   305	
   306		let rowCount = 0;
   307	
   308		for (const item of items) {
   309			rowCount = Math.max(rowCount, item.rowEnd);
   310			columnCount = Math.max(columnCount, item.columnEnd);
   311		}
   312	
   313		return {items, columnCount, rowCount};
   314	};
   315	
   316	type ResolveTrackSizesOptions = {
   317		tracks: Track[];
   318		items: GridItem[];
   319		trackCount: number;
   320		axis: 'column' | 'row';
   321		gap: number;
   322		availableSize: number;
   323		hasExplicitSize: boolean;
   324	};
   325	
   326	const resolveTrackSizes = (options: ResolveTrackSizesOptions): number[] => {
   327		const {tracks, items, trackCount, axis, gap, availableSize, hasExplicitSize} =
   328			options;
   329		const normalizedTracks: Track[] = [];
   330	
   331		for (let index = 0; index < trackCount; index++) {
   332			let track = tracks[index] ?? {kind: 'auto'};
   333	
   334			// `fr` tracks can't resolve against an auto-sized container.
   335			if (!hasExplicitSize && track.kind === 'fr') {
   336				track = {kind: 'auto'};
   337			}
   338	
   339			if (!hasExplicitSize && track.kind === 'minmax-fr') {
   340				track = {kind: 'minmax', min: track.min, max: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY};
   341			}
   342	
   343			normalizedTracks.push(track);
   344		}
   345	
   346		const sizes: number[] = normalizedTracks.map(track => {
   347			switch (track.kind) {
   348				case 'fixed': {
   349					return track.value;
   350				}
   351	
   352				case 'auto': {
   353					return 0;
   354				}
   355	
   356				case 'fr': {
   357					return 0;
   358				}
   359	
   360				case 'minmax': {

   434	const layoutGridContainer = (container: DOMElement): void => {
   435		const yoga = container.yogaNode;
   436		const {style} = container;
   437	
   438		if (!yoga) {
   439			return;
   440		}
   441

   443			.filter(child => isDomElement(child))
   444			.filter(child => Boolean(child.yogaNode))
   445			.filter(child => child.yogaNode!.getDisplay() !== Yoga.DISPLAY_NONE)
   446			.map(child => ({

   449				row: parseGridLine(child.style.gridRow),
   450				contentWidth: child.yogaNode!.getComputedWidth(),
   451				contentHeight: child.yogaNode!.getComputedHeight(),
   452				marginLeft: getMargin(child.style, 'left'),
   453				marginRight: getMargin(child.style, 'right'),

   456				columnStart: 0,

   459				rowEnd: 0,

   467		const rowsTemplate = parseTrackList(style.gridTemplateRows);
   468

   470		const {

   475

   477		const borderRight = yoga.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
   478		const borderTop = yoga.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
   479		const borderBottom = yoga.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);
   480		const paddingLeft = yoga.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
   481		const paddingRight = yoga.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
   482		const paddingTop = yoga.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
   483		const paddingBottom = yoga.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);
   484	
   485		const columnGap = style.columnGap ?? style.gap ?? 0;
   486		const rowGap = style.rowGap ?? style.gap ?? 0;
   487	
   488		const hasExplicitWidth = 'width' in style;
   489		const hasExplicitHeight = 'height' in style;
   490	
   491		const containerWidth = Math.max(
   492			0,
   493			yoga.getComputedWidth() -
   494				borderLeft -
   495				borderRight -

   497				paddingRight,

   499		const containerHeight = Math.max(
   500			0,

   509			tracks: columnsTemplate,
   510			items: placedItems,
   511			trackCount: columnCount,
   512			axis: 'column',
   513			gap: columnGap,
   514			availableSize: containerWidth,
   515			hasExplicitSize: hasExplicitWidth,
   516		});
   517	
   518		const rowSizes = resolveTrackSizes({
   519			tracks: rowsTemplate,
   520			items: placedItems,
   521			trackCount: rowCount,
   522			axis: 'row',
   523			gap: rowGap,
   524			availableSize: containerHeight,
   525			hasExplicitSize: hasExplicitHeight,
   526		});
   527	
   528		const totalColumnSize =
   529			columnSizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) +
   530			columnGap * (columnCount - 1);
   531		const totalRowSize =
   532			rowSizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) + rowGap * (rowCount - 1);
   533

   546		const columnSizeOffsets: number[] = [0];
   547		for (let index = 0; index < columnCount; index++) {
   548			columnSizeOffsets.push(columnSizeOffsets[index]! + columnSizes[index]!);
   549		}
   550	
   551		const rowSizeOffsets: number[] = [0];
   552		for (let index = 0; index < rowCount; index++) {
   553			rowSizeOffsets.push(rowSizeOffsets[index]! + rowSizes[index]!);
   554		}
   555	
   556		const columnOffsets = columnSizeOffsets.map(
   557			(sizeOffset, index) => sizeOffset + columnGap * index,
   558		);
   559		const rowOffsets = rowSizeOffsets.map(
   560			(sizeOffset, index) => sizeOffset + rowGap * index,
   561		);
   562	
   563		for (const item of placedItems) {
   564			const childYoga = item.child.yogaNode!;
   565			const width =
   566				columnSizeOffsets[item.columnEnd]! -
   567				columnSizeOffsets[item.columnStart]! +
   568				columnGap * (item.columnEnd - item.columnStart - 1);
   569			const height =
   570				rowSizeOffsets[item.rowEnd]! -
   571				rowSizeOffsets[item.rowStart]! +
   572				rowGap * (item.rowEnd - item.rowStart - 1);
   573	
   574			childYoga.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE);
   575			childYoga.setPosition(
   576				Yoga.EDGE_LEFT,
   577				paddingLeft + columnOffsets[item.columnStart]!,
   578			);
   579			childYoga.setPosition(
   580				Yoga.EDGE_TOP,
   581				paddingTop + rowOffsets[item.rowStart]!,
   582			);
   583			childYoga.setWidth(Math.max(0, width - item.marginLeft - item.marginRight));
   584			childYoga.setHeight(
   585				Math.max(0, height - item.marginTop - item.marginBottom),
   586			);
   587		}
   588	};
   589	
   590	const computeGridLayouts = (node: DOMElement): void => {
   591		if (isGridContainer(node)) {
   592			layoutGridContainer(node);
   593		}
   594	
   595		for (const child of node.childNodes) {
   596			if (isDomElement(child) && child.yogaNode) {
   597				computeGridLayouts(child);
   598			}
   599		}
   600	};
   601	
   602	export const applyGridLayout = (rootNode: DOMElement): void => {
   603		const rootYoga = rootNode.yogaNode;
   604	
   605		if (!rootYoga) {
   606			return;
   607		}
   608	
   609		// Ensure grid children are measured at their natural content size before
   610		// grid tracks are resolved. Reset any explicit placement from a previous
   611		// layout so auto tracks don't grow on subsequent renders.
   612		for (const container of collectGridContainers(rootNode)) {
   613			const children = container.childNodes
   614				.filter(child => isDomElement(child))
   615				.filter(child => Boolean(child.yogaNode));
   616	
   617			for (const child of children) {
   618				const childYoga = child.yogaNode!;
   619				childYoga.setFlexShrink(0);
   620				childYoga.setFlexGrow(0);
   621				childYoga.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_RELATIVE);
   622				childYoga.setPosition(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT, 0);
   623				childYoga.setPosition(Yoga.EDGE_TOP, 0);
   624	
   625				if (typeof child.style.width === 'number') {
   626					childYoga.setWidth(child.style.width);
   627				} else {
   628					childYoga.setWidthAuto();
   629				}
   630	
   631				if (typeof child.style.height === 'number') {
   632					childYoga.setHeight(child.style.height);
   633				} else {
   634					childYoga.setHeightAuto();
   635				}
   636			}
   637		}
   638	
   639		rootYoga.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
   640	
   641		// Apply the grid layout and recalculate so explicitly positioned children
   642		// stay in place and auto-sized containers get their final dimensions.
   643		computeGridLayouts(rootNode);
   644		rootYoga.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
   645		computeGridLayouts(rootNode);
   646		rootYoga.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
   647	};
   648	
   649	export default applyGridLayout;
